[[instructions]]
== Instructions
:imagesdir: ../images
:source-highlighter: rouge




AME defines *124* instructions.

[IMPORTANT]
====
The per-instruction `Operation` blocks below are expanded illustrations of the
instruction-specific dataflow.  They are interpreted together with the common
rules in <<programming_model>>, including operand validation and exception
priority, complete register-group formation, `N_sq` logical-square expansion,
logical element access, and staged atomic register writeback.  When an expanded
block shows a one-square loop or direct per-register indexing, the common rules
are normative and supply the complete behavior for packed and wide datatypes;
the shorthand does not restrict execution to one square or permit partial
register-group access.

Before any AME instruction other than `ame.acquire` or `ame.release` executes
its `Operation` block, the implementation performs
`<<ame:doc:func:ame_check_state_access,ame_check_state_access>>`. This check has
priority over all instruction-specific validation.
====

include::instruction-encoding-allocations.adoc[]

[#ame:doc:inst:ame_acquire]
=== `ame.acquire`

Synopsis::
Acquire the entire AME backend

Mnemonic::
`ame.acquire xd, xs`

Encoding::
This specification does not assign an instruction encoding.

Description::
`ame.acquire` atomically requests the entire backend. `xs` contains an
acquisition descriptor and `xd` receives an acquisition response.
+
The descriptor has the following layout:
+
[cols="1,1,3",options="header"]
|===
| Field | Location | Description

| WAIT_MODE
| `1:0`
| Waiting policy

| TIMEOUT_CLASS
| `5:2`
| Bounded-wait class

| RESERVED
| `XLEN-1:6`
| Must be zero
|===
+
`WAIT_MODE=0b00` is TRY and does not wait.
`WAIT_MODE=0b01` is BOUNDED_WAIT and may wait for a finite interval.
The other encodings are reserved. `TIMEOUT_CLASS` is ignored for TRY. For
BOUNDED_WAIT, `0x0`, `0x1`, `0x2`, and `0x3` select SHORT, MEDIUM, LONG, and
VERY_LONG; `0xF` is implementation-defined; all other values are reserved.
The duration of each class is implementation-defined, but every bounded wait
must be finite.
+
The response has the following layout:
+
[cols="1,1,3",options="header"]
|===
| Field | Location | Description

| SUCCESS
| `0`
| One on success; zero on failure

| STATUS
| `7:1`
| Acquisition status

| RESERVED
| `XLEN-1:8`
| Zero
|===
+
[cols="1,2,1,4",options="header",%unbreakable]
|===
| STATUS | Name | SUCCESS | Description

| `0x00`
| GRANTED
| 1
| Granted or already owned

| `0x01`
| BUSY
| 0
| The backend is not currently available

| `0x02`
| UNSUPPORTED
| 0
| No AME backend is accessible

| `0x03`
| DISABLED
| 0
| Disabled by `MS` or configuration

| `0x04`
| BAD_DESC
| 0
| The descriptor is invalid

| `0x05`
| TIMEOUT
| 0
| A bounded wait expired

| `0x06`
| INTERRUPTED
| 0
| Wait interrupted by an interrupt or trap

| `0x07`
| DENIED
| 0
| Denied by platform policy

| `0x08-0x7E`
| RESERVED
| -
| Reserved

| `0x7F`
| IMPL_DEFINED
| Impl.
| Implementation-defined result
|===
+
An invalid descriptor returns BAD_DESC, and disabled AME returns DISABLED.
After these checks, a request from the current owner is a no-op success that
preserves the bound state and `MS`. Otherwise, GRANTED binds architectural
initial state, sets `ameown.OWNED` to one, and sets every applicable `MS` field
to Initial as one atomic architectural action.
+
A failed acquire leaves the backend binding, `ameown`, and `MS` unchanged.

Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
X[xd] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_acquire_backend,ame_acquire_backend>>pass:[(]X[xs]);
----

[#ame:doc:inst:ame_release]
=== `ame.release`

Synopsis::
Release the entire AME backend

Mnemonic::
`ame.release`

Encoding::
This specification does not assign an instruction encoding.

Description::
`ame.release` is legal regardless of `MS`. If the current context owns the
backend, the instruction stops accepting new AME operations from that context,
completes its retired operations and earlier state-save stores, unbinds its
backend state, and atomically releases ownership.
+
After releasing an owned backend, `ameown.OWNED` is zero and every applicable
non-Off `MS` field is Initial. If the current context does not own the backend,
the instruction is a legal no-op that preserves `MS`.

Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
<<ame:doc:func:ame_release_backend,ame_release_backend>>pass:[(]);
----


<<<

[#ame:doc:inst:agettyp]
=== `agettyp`

Synopsis::
Get accumulator datatype

Mnemonic::
`agettyp xd, ad`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xd","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ad","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0x0,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Get the datatype of an `Acc` register by reading from `Ad`.



Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> xd = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> ad = $encoding[19:15];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
<<ame:doc:func:ame_check_acc_index,ame_check_acc_index>>pass:[(]ad, $encoding);
X[xd] = Ad[ad];
----


<<<

[#ame:doc:inst:asettyp]
=== `asettyp`

Synopsis::
Set accumulator datatype

Mnemonic::
`asettyp ad, xs1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ad","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0x1,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Sets the datatype of an `Acc` register and zeros the data by writing to `Ad` and zeroing `Acc`.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
acc = np.zeros_like(acc)
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> ad = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
<<ame:doc:func:ame_check_acc_index,ame_check_acc_index>>pass:[(]ad, $encoding);
Bits<32> dtype = X[xs1][31:0];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_datatype_supported,ame_datatype_supported>>pass:[(]dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  Ad[ad] = X[xs1];
  Acc[ad] = 0;
}
----


<<<

[#ame:doc:inst:mabs_ew]
=== `mabs.ew`

Synopsis::

Mnemonic::

=== `mabsdiff.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise scalar absolute difference

Mnemonic::
`mabsdiff.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x1,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Computes the elementwise absolute difference between a scalar constant from integer
register `xs1` and every element of square `ms2`, stored into `md`.
+
The type of the scalar is determined by `CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`. The scalar is converted
to the datatype of `ms2` before the operation and then applied to every element position `i`:
+
  `md[i] = |xs1 - ms2[i]|`
+
If either `Md[md]` or `Md[ms2]` is uninitialized (zero), or the datatype combination
is not supported, `amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md = np.abs(xs1 - ms2)
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----

Bits<32> src_dtype = Md[ms2];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_scalar_op_supported,ame_scalar_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::ABSDIFF1D, dest_dtype, src_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src_dtype);
  U32 src_nregs = src_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src_nregs == 0) {
    src_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms2 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar = X[xs1];
  for pass:[(]U32 e = 0; e < AME_NELEM; e++) {
    U32 src_regno = e / src_elements_per_reg;
    Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> src_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms2 pass:[+] src_regno], src_dtype);
    U32 src_hi_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src_element_size) - (src_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - 1;
    U32 src_lo_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src_element_size) - (src_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - src_element_size;
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src_element_value = src_value[src_hi_idx:src_lo_idx];

=== `mand.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise scalar bitwise AND

Mnemonic::
`mand.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.

with every element of square `ms2`, stored into `md`.

to the datatype of `ms2` before the operation and then applied to every element position `i`:

is not supported, `amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written.

[#ame:doc:inst:mcmovge_ew]
=== `mcmovge.ew`

Synopsis::
Elementwise two-way conditional move (non-negative)

Mnemonic::
`mcmovge.ew md, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x2a,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Elementwise two-way conditional move using the non-negative sign of `ms1` as predicate.
+
For each element, `md[i] = ms2[i]` if `ms1[i] >= 0` (sign bit clear),
otherwise `md[i]` is left unchanged.
+
`md` is both a source and destination: when the predicate is false (sign bit set),
the existing element in `md` is preserved.  `ms1` serves as the predicate register:
any non-negative value is treated as true.
+
Complementary to `mcmovlt.ew`.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md = np.where(ms1 >= 0, ms2, md)
----

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]

Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];

[#ame:doc:inst:mcmovlt_ew]
=== `mcmovlt.ew`

Synopsis::
Elementwise two-way conditional move (negative)

Mnemonic::
`mcmovlt.ew md, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Elementwise two-way conditional move using the sign of `ms1` as predicate.
+
For each element, `md[i] = ms2[i]` if `ms1[i] < 0` (sign bit set),
otherwise `md[i]` is left unchanged.
+
`md` is both a source and destination: when the predicate is false (sign bit clear),
the existing element in `md` is preserved.  `ms1` serves as the predicate register:
any negative value is treated as true.
+
Complementary to `mcmovge.ew`.

md = np.where(ms1 < 0, ms2, md)

=== `mcmpge.ew`

Synopsis::
Elementwise greater-or-equal compare

Mnemonic::
`mcmpge.ew md, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::

[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.

Elementwise greater-or-equal comparison of the tensors in `ms1` and `ms2`, stored into `md`.

For each element, `md[i]` is set to the canonical true value (-1, all bits set) if

in the presence of NaN, +∞, and −∞.

[#ame:doc:inst:mcmpge_ew_x]
=== `mcmpge.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise scalar compare greater-or-equal

Mnemonic::
`mcmpge.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x2d,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Compares a scalar constant from integer register `xs1` against every element of square
`ms2`, storing -1 (all bits set) where the scalar is greater than or equal to the element,
and 0 otherwise, into `md`.
+
The type of the scalar is determined by `CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`. The scalar is converted
to the datatype of `ms2` before the operation and then applied to every element position `i`:
+
  `md[i] = (xs1 >= ms2[i]) ? -1 : 0`
+
If either `Md[md]` or `Md[ms2]` is uninitialized (zero), or the datatype combination
is not supported, `amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written.

  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;

[#ame:doc:inst:mcmplt_ew_x]
=== `mcmplt.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise scalar compare less-than

Mnemonic::
`mcmplt.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x2f,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Compares a scalar constant from integer register `xs1` against every element of square
`ms2`, storing -1 (all bits set) where the scalar is strictly less than the element,
and 0 otherwise, into `md`.
+
The type of the scalar is determined by `CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`. The scalar is converted
to the datatype of `ms2` before the operation and then applied to every element position `i`:
+
  `md[i] = (xs1 < ms2[i]) ? -1 : 0`
+
If either `Md[md]` or `Md[ms2]` is uninitialized (zero), or the datatype combination
is not supported, `amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md = (xs1 < ms2).astype(md.dtype) * -1
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<32> dest_dtype = Md[md];
Bits<32> src_dtype = Md[ms2];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_scalar_op_supported,ame_scalar_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::CMPLT1D, dest_dtype, src_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src_dtype);
  U32 src_nregs = src_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src_nregs == 0) {
    src_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms2 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar = X[xs1];
  for pass:[(]U32 e = 0; e < AME_NELEM; e++) {
    U32 src_regno = e / src_elements_per_reg;
    Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> src_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms2 pass:[+] src_regno], src_dtype);
    U32 src_hi_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src_element_size) - (src_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - 1;
    U32 src_lo_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src_element_size) - (src_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - src_element_size;
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src_element_value = src_value[src_hi_idx:src_lo_idx];
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar_element_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_scalar_data_value,ame_scalar_data_value>>pass:[(]src_dtype, scalar);
    <<ame:doc:func:ame_stage_m_element_write,ame_stage_m_element_write>>pass:[(]md, dest_dtype, e, <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::CMPLT1D, src_dtype, scalar_element_value, src_dtype, src_element_value, dest_dtype));
  }
}

[#ame:doc:inst:mcolgather_ew]
=== `mcolgather.ew`

Synopsis::
Elementwise column gather (take)

Mnemonic::
`mcolgather.ew md, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x33,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Elementwise gather: for each element in each row, uses the corresponding index from `ms2`
to select a column from the same row of `ms1`, and stores the result into `md`.
+
For each row `i` and column `j`:
+
  `md[i,j] = ms1[i, ms2[i,j]]`
+
`ms2` provides integer column indices in the range `[0, sqrt(AME_NELEM))`.
An out-of-range index raises an `Illegal Instruction` exception.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md = np.take_along_axis(ms1, ms2, axis=1)
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----

Bits<32> src1_dtype = Md[ms1];
Bits<32> src2_dtype = Md[ms2];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::GATHER1D, dest_dtype, src1_dtype, src1_dtype) ||
    !<<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_integer,ame_dtype_is_integer>>pass:[(]src2_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src1_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src1_dtype);
  U32 src1_nregs = src1_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src1_nregs == 0) {
    src1_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src1_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src1_nregs;
  U32 src2_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src2_dtype);
  U32 src2_nregs = src2_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src2_nregs == 0) {
    src2_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src2_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src2_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 dest_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / dest_nregs;
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || ms2 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms1 % src1_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src2_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  U32 N = <<ame:doc:func:sqrt,sqrt>>pass:[(]AME_NELEM);
  for pass:[(]U32 dst_regno = 0; dst_regno < dest_nregs; dst_regno++) {
    Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> result = 0;
    for pass:[(]U32 local_e = 0; local_e < dest_elements_per_reg; local_e++) {
      U32 e = dst_regno * dest_elements_per_reg pass:[+] local_e;

      U32 idx_hi = idx_lo pass:[+] src2_element_size - 1;

      U32 src_regno = e_src / src1_elements_per_reg;
      U32 src_lo = e_src * src1_element_size - (src_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE);
      U32 src_hi = src_lo pass:[+] src1_element_size - 1;
      U32 dst_lo = local_e * dest_element_size;
      U32 dst_hi = dst_lo pass:[+] dest_element_size - 1;
      Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms1 pass:[+] src_regno], src1_dtype)[src_hi:src_lo];
      result[dst_hi:dst_lo] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::GATHER1D, src1_dtype, src_value, src1_dtype, src_value, dest_dtype);

[#ame:doc:inst:mcolshift_ew_x]
=== `mcolshift.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise column shift by scalar offset

Mnemonic::
`mcolshift.ew.x md, ms1, xs1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x34,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Elementwise column shift of `ms1` by a signed 32-bit integer offset `c` from integer
register `xs1`, stored into `md`.  This fixed-type control operand does not read
`CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`.
+
For each row `i` and column `j`:
+
  `md[i,j] = ms1[i, j + c]`  if `0 <= j + c < sqrt(AME_NELEM)`, otherwise
  `md[i,j] = zero(Md[md])`.
+
Elements shifted beyond the row boundary are filled with zero (no wrap-around).
For the row-shift form, see `mrowshift.ew.x`.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md = np.zeros_like(ms1)
if 0 <= c < ms1.shape[1]:
    md[:, :ms1.shape[1] - c] = ms1[:, c:]
elif -ms1.shape[1] < c < 0:
    md[:, -c:] = ms1[:, :ms1.shape[1] + c]
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[24:20];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----

Bits<32> src1_dtype = Md[ms1];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::SHIFT1D, dest_dtype, src1_dtype, src1_dtype) ||
    !<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::ZERO1D, dest_dtype, dest_dtype, dest_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src1_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src1_dtype);
  U32 src1_nregs = src1_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src1_nregs == 0) {
    src1_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src1_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src1_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 dest_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / dest_nregs;
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms1 % src1_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  U32 N = <<ame:doc:func:sqrt,sqrt>>pass:[(]AME_NELEM);
  Bits<32> shift = $signed(X[xs1][31:0]);
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> zero_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::ZERO1D, dest_dtype, 0, dest_dtype, 0, dest_dtype);
  for pass:[(]U32 dst_regno = 0; dst_regno < dest_nregs; dst_regno++) {
    Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> result = 0;
    for pass:[(]U32 local_e = 0; local_e < dest_elements_per_reg; local_e++) {
      U32 e_out = dst_regno * dest_elements_per_reg pass:[+] local_e;
      U32 i = e_out / N;
      U32 j = e_out % N;

      U32 dst_hi = dst_lo pass:[+] dest_element_size - 1;
      result[dst_hi:dst_lo] = zero_value;

        Bits<32> src_regno = $signed+++(+++e_src) / src1_elements_per_reg;
        U32 src_lo = e_src * src1_element_size - ($signed+++(+++src_regno) * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE);
        U32 src_hi = src_lo pass:[+] src1_element_size - 1;
        Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms1 pass:[+] src_regno], src1_dtype)[src_hi:src_lo];
        result[dst_hi:dst_lo] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::SHIFT1D, src1_dtype, src_value, src1_dtype, src_value, dest_dtype);
      }

    M[md pass:[+] dst_regno] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_rm_to_impl,ame_xform_rm_to_impl>>pass:[(]result, dest_dtype);
  }

[#ame:doc:inst:mcolunzip_ew]
=== `mcolunzip.ew`

Synopsis::
Elementwise column unzip (de-interleave)

Mnemonic::
`mcolunzip.ew md, ms1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0xa,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}
....

Description::
De-interleaves each row of `ms1` into lower and upper halves in `md`.
+
Let `N = sqrt(AME_NELEM)`. For each row `i` and index `j` in `[0, N/2)`:
+
  `md[i, j]       = ms1[i, 2j]`
  `md[i, j + N/2] = ms1[i, 2j+1]`
+
Even-indexed columns of each row are collected into the lower half of the output;
odd-indexed columns into the upper half.  Inverse of `mcolzip.ew`.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md = np.concatenate((ms1[:, 0::2], ms1[:, 1::2]), axis=1)
----

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]

Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
----

Bits<32> src1_dtype = Md[ms1];

  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src1_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src1_dtype);
  U32 src1_nregs = src1_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src1_nregs == 0) {
    src1_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src1_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src1_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms1 % src1_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  U32 N = <<ame:doc:func:sqrt,sqrt>>pass:[(]AME_NELEM);
  for pass:[(]U32 i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    for pass:[(]U32 j = 0; j < N / 2; j++) {
      U32 e_src_even = i * N pass:[+] 2 * j;
      U32 e_dst_lo = i * N pass:[+] j;
      U32 src_even_regno = e_src_even / src1_elements_per_reg;
      U32 src_even_hi = ((e_src_even pass:[+] 1) * src1_element_size) - (src_even_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) - 1;
      U32 src_even_lo = ((e_src_even pass:[+] 1) * src1_element_size) - (src_even_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) - src1_element_size;
      <<ame:doc:func:ame_stage_m_element_write,ame_stage_m_element_write>>pass:[(]md, dest_dtype, e_dst_lo, <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::COLUNZIP1D, src1_dtype, <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms1 pass:[+] src_even_regno], src1_dtype)[src_even_hi:src_even_lo], src1_dtype, <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms1 pass:[+] src_even_regno], src1_dtype)[src_even_hi:src_even_lo], dest_dtype));
      U32 e_src_odd = i * N pass:[+] 2 * j pass:[+] 1;

Synopsis::
Elementwise column zip (interleave)

Mnemonic::
`mcolzip.ew md, ms1`

Encoding::
+

+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0xb,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}

+

+
  `md[i, 2j]   = ms1[i, j]`
  `md[i, 2j+1] = ms1[i, j + N/2]`
+
The lower half of each row occupies even columns in the output; the upper half
occupies odd columns.  Inverse of `mcolunzip.ew`.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
half = ms1.shape[1] // 2
md = np.empty_like(ms1)
md[:, 0::2] = ms1[:, :half]
md[:, 1::2] = ms1[:, half:]
----

[#ame:doc:inst:mconv_ew]
=== `mconv.ew`

Synopsis::
Convert element data between supported datatypes, including packed and non-packed representations

Mnemonic::
`mconv.ew md, ms1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0xd,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Converts element data between a packed datatype and a non-packed datatype.
+
The source datatype is determined from `Md[ms1]` and the destination datatype from `Md[md]`.
Exactly one of the two must be a packed datatype (size < `AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE`);
if both or neither are packed, `mconv.ew` sets `amestatus.UN` and returns without
modifying any register.
+
**Expand (packed → non-packed):** When `Md[ms1]` is packed and `Md[md]` is a
non-packed datatype, `ms1` holds `pack_factor = AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / sizeof(Md[ms1])`
packed squares. `mconv.ew` converts each element from the packed datatype to the non-packed
datatype and writes `pack_factor` consecutive result-square groups.  If the
destination square occupies `dst_group_size` registers, square `sq` begins at
`md + sq * dst_group_size`.  The base must be aligned to `dst_group_size`, and
the complete `pack_factor * dst_group_size` span must fit in the M register
file; otherwise, `mconv.ew` raises an `Illegal Instruction` exception.
+
**Compress (non-packed → packed):** When `Md[ms1]` is a non-packed datatype and `Md[md]`
is packed, `pack_factor` consecutive non-packed square groups begin at `ms1`.
If each source square occupies `src_group_size` registers, square `sq` begins
at `ms1 + sq * src_group_size`.
`mconv.ew` converts each element from the non-packed datatype to the packed datatype and packs
all `pack_factor` result squares into the single register `md`.
The base must be aligned to `src_group_size`, and the complete
`pack_factor * src_group_size` span must fit; otherwise, `mconv.ew` raises an
`Illegal Instruction` exception.
+
The element-level conversion follows the common `CONV1D` contract in
`<<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>`; unsupported conversion tuples set
`amestatus.UN` and do not modify any register.



Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<32> src_dtype = Md[ms1];
Bits<32> dst_dtype = Md[md];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::CONV1D, dst_dtype, src_dtype, src_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else if pass:[(]<<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_packed,ame_dtype_is_packed>>pass:[(]src_dtype) == <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_packed,ame_dtype_is_packed>>pass:[(]dst_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(]<<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_packed,ame_dtype_is_packed>>pass:[(]src_dtype)) {
    U32 src_element_size_bits = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src_dtype);
    U32 pack_factor = AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / src_element_size_bits;
    U32 dst_element_size_bits = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dst_dtype);
    U32 dst_group_size = dst_element_size_bits / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
    if pass:[(]dst_group_size == 0) {
      dst_group_size = 1;
    }
    <<ame:doc:func:ame_check_m_group,ame_check_m_group>>pass:[(]ms1, 1, 1, $encoding);
    <<ame:doc:func:ame_check_m_group,ame_check_m_group>>pass:[(]md, dst_group_size, pack_factor * dst_group_size, $encoding);
    for pass:[(]U32 sq = 0; sq < pack_factor; sq++) {
      for pass:[(]U32 e = 0; e < AME_NELEM; e++) {
        Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> element = <<ame:doc:func:ame_read_m_element,ame_read_m_element>>pass:[(]ms1, src_dtype, sq * AME_NELEM pass:[+] e);
        Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> converted = <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::CONV1D, src_dtype, element, src_dtype, element, dst_dtype);
        <<ame:doc:func:ame_stage_m_element_write,ame_stage_m_element_write>>pass:[(]md pass:[+] sq * dst_group_size, dst_dtype, e, converted);
      }
    }
  } else {
    U32 dst_element_size_bits = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dst_dtype);
    U32 pack_factor = AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / dst_element_size_bits;
    U32 src_element_size_bits = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src_dtype);
    U32 src_group_size = src_element_size_bits / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
    if pass:[(]src_group_size == 0) {
      src_group_size = 1;
    }
    <<ame:doc:func:ame_check_m_group,ame_check_m_group>>pass:[(]ms1, src_group_size, pack_factor * src_group_size, $encoding);
    <<ame:doc:func:ame_check_m_group,ame_check_m_group>>pass:[(]md, 1, 1, $encoding);
    Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> packed = 0;
    for pass:[(]U32 sq = 0; sq < pack_factor; sq++) {
      for pass:[(]U32 e = 0; e < AME_NELEM; e++) {
        Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> element = <<ame:doc:func:ame_read_m_element,ame_read_m_element>>pass:[(]ms1 pass:[+] sq * src_group_size, src_dtype, e);
        U32 dst_lo = (sq * AME_NELEM pass:[+] e) * dst_element_size_bits;
        U32 dst_hi = dst_lo pass:[+] dst_element_size_bits - 1;
        packed[dst_hi:dst_lo] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::CONV1D, src_dtype, element, src_dtype, element, dst_dtype);
      }
    }
    M[md] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_rm_to_impl,ame_xform_rm_to_impl>>pass:[(]packed, dst_dtype);
  }
}

[#ame:doc:inst:mmov_m_x]
=== `mmov.m.x`

Synopsis::
Convert an X-register scalar and broadcast it to an M operand

Mnemonic::
`mmov.m.x md, xs1, xtyp`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name":0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name":"md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name":{ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name":"xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name":"xtyp","type":4},{"bits":7,"name":0x32,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Interprets the low 32 bits of `X[xtyp]` as an AME datatype encoding and the
low `ame_dtype_val_to_size(src_dtype)` bits of `X[xs1]` as one scalar of that datatype, where
`src_dtype = X[xtyp][31:0]`.  The
scalar is converted once to the destination datatype `Md[md]` using the
common `CONV1D` contract, then the converted value is written to every
logical element of `M[md]`.  Packed destinations receive the value in every
logical packed element.
+
The source and destination datatype encodings must be supported, and the
source-to-destination conversion must be supported.  Otherwise,
`amestatus.UN` is set and no destination state is modified.  The instruction
does not read `CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`; the source datatype is its explicit
`xtyp` operand.

NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md[:] = broadcast(convert(X[xs1], source_dtype=src_dtype, target_dtype=Md[md]))
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> xtyp = $encoding[24:20];
----

Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16 && (md >= 16)) {
  <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
}
Bits<32> dest_dtype = Md[md];
Bits<32> src_dtype = X[xtyp][31:0];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_datatype_supported,ame_datatype_supported>>pass:[(]dest_dtype) ||
    !<<ame:doc:func:ame_datatype_supported,ame_datatype_supported>>pass:[(]src_dtype) ||
  !<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::CONV1D, dest_dtype, src_dtype, src_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_group_size = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_group_size == 0) {
    dest_group_size = 1;
  }
  <<ame:doc:func:ame_check_m_group,ame_check_m_group>>pass:[(]md, dest_group_size, dest_group_size, $encoding);
  U32 src_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src_dtype);
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> raw_scalar = X[xs1];
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> converted = <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::CONV1D, src_dtype, raw_scalar[src_element_size - 1:0], src_dtype, 0, dest_dtype);
  U32 total_elements = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_packed,ame_dtype_is_packed>>pass:[(]dest_dtype) ? (AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / dest_element_size) * AME_NELEM : AME_NELEM;
  for pass:[(]U32 e = 0; e < total_elements; e++) {
    <<ame:doc:func:ame_stage_m_element_write,ame_stage_m_element_write>>pass:[(]md, dest_dtype, e, converted);
  }
}
----

[#ame:doc:inst:mcos_ew]
=== `mcos.ew`

Synopsis::
Elementwise cosine

Mnemonic::
`mcos.ew md, ms1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0xe,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Elementwise cosine of `ms1`, stored into `md`.
+
This instruction is defined only for floating-point datatypes. If the datatype of
`md` or `ms1` is not a supported floating-point type, `amestatus.UN` is set and no
register is written.
+
The numerical precision of the result is implementation-defined.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md = np.cos(ms1)
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<32> dest_dtype = Md[md];
Bits<32> src1_dtype = Md[ms1];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::COS1D, dest_dtype, src1_dtype, 0)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src1_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src1_dtype);
  U32 src1_nregs = src1_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src1_nregs == 0) {
    src1_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src1_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src1_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 dest_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / dest_nregs;
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms1 % src1_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  for pass:[(]U32 dest_regno = 0; dest_regno < dest_nregs; dest_regno++) {
    Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> result = 0;
    for pass:[(]U32 local_e = 0; local_e < dest_elements_per_reg; local_e++) {
      U32 e = dest_regno * dest_elements_per_reg pass:[+] local_e;
      U32 src1_regno = e / src1_elements_per_reg;
      Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> src1_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms1 pass:[+] src1_regno], src1_dtype);
      U32 src1_lo_idx = e * src1_element_size - (src1_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE);
      U32 src1_hi_idx = src1_lo_idx pass:[+] src1_element_size - 1;
      U32 dest_lo_idx = local_e * dest_element_size;
      U32 dest_hi_idx = dest_lo_idx pass:[+] dest_element_size - 1;
      Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src1_element_value = src1_value[src1_hi_idx:src1_lo_idx];
      result[dest_hi_idx:dest_lo_idx] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::COS1D, src1_dtype, src1_element_value, src1_dtype, src1_element_value, dest_dtype);
    }
    M[md pass:[+] dest_regno] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_rm_to_impl,ame_xform_rm_to_impl>>pass:[(]result, dest_dtype);
  }
}

Synopsis::

Mnemonic::

Encoding::

[IMPORTANT]

[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0xf,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}

  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms1 % src1_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {

[#ame:doc:inst:mhdiff_ew_x]
=== `mhdiff.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise scalar half-difference

Mnemonic::
`mhdiff.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x5,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Computes the elementwise half-difference between a scalar constant from integer register
`xs1` and every element of square `ms2`, stored into `md`.
+
The type of the scalar is determined by `CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`. The scalar is converted
to the datatype of `ms2` before the operation and then applied to every element position `i`:
+
  `md[i] = (xs1 - ms2[i]) / 2`
+
If either `Md[md]` or `Md[ms2]` is uninitialized (zero), or the datatype combination
is not supported, `amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written.

  U32 src_nregs = src_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;

  U32 src_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;

=== `mldexp.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise scalar left-shift scale (ldexp)

Mnemonic::
`mldexp.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x40,"type":2}]}

Description::
Scales every element of square `ms2` by a power of two given by a scalar integer
from integer register `xs1`, stored into `md`.
+
For each element position `i`:

interpreted as an integer of the maximum supported AME integer datatype (`AME_MAX_INT_DTYPE`).
The exponent is a fixed-type control operand and does not read `CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`.

+
If either `Md[md]` or `Md[ms2]` is uninitialized (zero), or the datatype combination
is not supported, `amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written.

Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----

  U32 src_nregs = src_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src_nregs == 0) {
    src_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 exp_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]exp_dtype);
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms2 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {

Synopsis::
Elementwise left-shift scale accumulate

Mnemonic::
`mldexpacc.ew md, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::

[#ame:doc:inst:mlog2sub_ew_x]
=== `mlog2sub.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise log2 minus scalar bias

Mnemonic::
`mlog2sub.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x44,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Computes the elementwise base-2 logarithm of the absolute value of every element of
square `ms2`, minus a scalar bias from integer register `xs1`, stored into `md`.
+
The type of the scalar is determined by `CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`. The scalar is converted
to the datatype of `ms2` before being used as the bias for every element position `i`:
+
  `md[i] = log2(|ms2[i]|) - xs1`
+
Useful for biased-exponent arithmetic, where `xs1` holds the bias value.
Complementary to `msublog2.ew.x`.
+
If either `Md[md]` or `Md[ms2]` is uninitialized (zero), or the datatype combination
is not supported, `amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written.

  U32 src_nregs = src_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src_nregs == 0) {

  U32 src_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;

    }

[#ame:doc:inst:mls]
=== `mls`

Synopsis::
Load raw M register from implementation-defined format

Mnemonic::
`mls md, xs1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0x16,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Loads exactly one physical M register from memory in the implementation-defined
register format into matrix register `md`.
+
The data in memory must have been previously stored in the implementation-defined
register format (e.g., by a matching `mss` instruction). No datatype conversion is
performed; the in-memory layout is the exact physical-register representation.
The datatype in `Md[md]` is not interpreted.  Consequently, packed sub-squares
resident in `md` are transferred together, while a wide logical square requires
one `mls` for each physical M register in its group.
+
For portable interoperability with other implementations, use
<<ame:doc:inst:mls_rm,mls.rm>> or <<ame:doc:inst:mls_cm,mls.cm>>.
+
The address must be aligned to the physical M-register size or 4KB,
whichever is smaller. Otherwise, `mls` raises a `Load Misaligned` exception.



Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
XReg address = X[xs1];
if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
  if pass:[(]md >= 16) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
}
XReg impl_size_bytes = AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE >> 3;
XReg alignment = <<ame:doc:func:min,min>>pass:[(]impl_size_bytes, 4096);
if pass:[(]address % alignment != 0) {
  <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::LoadAddressMisaligned, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), address);
}
M[md] = <<ame:doc:func:read_memory_ame,read_memory_ame>>pass:[(]address, $encoding, impl_size_bytes);
----


<<<

[#ame:doc:inst:mls_cm]
=== `mls.cm`

Synopsis::
Load square from column-major format

Mnemonic::
`mls.cm md, xs1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0x17,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Loads a square from memory in column-major format into matrix register `md`.
+
The datatype is determined from `Md[md]`. For compute datatypes
(size >= `AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE`), one square is loaded. For packed datatypes
(size < `AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE`), `pack_factor = AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / sizeof(Md[md])`
squares are loaded and packed into a single M register.
+
The data in memory is stored contiguously in column-major order: within each sub-square,
column `j` immediately follows column `j-1` with no padding. All elements are loaded
unconditionally.
+
The address must be aligned to the total data size
(`pack_factor * AME_NELEM * sizeof(Md[md])`) or 4KB, whichever is smaller.
Otherwise, `mls.cm` raises a `Load Misaligned` exception.



Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
XReg address = X[xs1];
if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
  if pass:[(]md >= 16) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
}
Bits<32> dtype = Md[md];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_datatype_supported,ame_datatype_supported>>pass:[(]dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
U32 element_size_bits = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dtype);
U32 pack_factor = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_packed,ame_dtype_is_packed>>pass:[(]dtype) ? (AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / element_size_bits) : 1;
U32 group_size = (element_size_bits pass:[+] AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE - 1) / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
if pass:[(](md % group_size) != 0 || (md pass:[+] group_size) > AME_NUM_M_REGS) {
  <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
}
XReg total_bytes = (pack_factor * AME_NELEM * element_size_bits pass:[+] 7) / 8;
XReg alignment = <<ame:doc:func:min,min>>pass:[(]total_bytes, 4096);
if pass:[(]address % alignment != 0) {
  <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::LoadAddressMisaligned, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), address);
}

[#ame:doc:inst:mls_rm]
=== `mls.rm`

Synopsis::
Load square from row-major format

Mnemonic::
`mls.rm md, xs1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0x18,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Loads a square from memory in row-major format into matrix register `md`.
+
The datatype is determined from `Md[md]`. For compute datatypes
(size >= `AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE`), one square is loaded. For packed datatypes
(size < `AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE`), `pack_factor = AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / sizeof(Md[md])`
squares are loaded and packed into a single M register.
+
The data in memory is stored contiguously in row-major order: within each sub-square,
row `i` immediately follows row `i-1` with no padding. All elements are loaded
unconditionally.
+
The address must be aligned to the total data size
(`pack_factor * AME_NELEM * sizeof(Md[md])`) or 4KB, whichever is smaller.
Otherwise, `mls.rm` raises a `Load Misaligned` exception.

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]

Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
----

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]

if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
  if pass:[(]md >= 16) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
}
Bits<32> dtype = Md[md];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_datatype_supported,ame_datatype_supported>>pass:[(]dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
U32 element_size_bits = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dtype);
U32 pack_factor = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_packed,ame_dtype_is_packed>>pass:[(]dtype) ? (AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / element_size_bits) : 1;
U32 group_size = (element_size_bits pass:[+] AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE - 1) / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
if pass:[(](md % group_size) != 0 || (md pass:[+] group_size) > AME_NUM_M_REGS) {
  <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
}
XReg total_bytes = (pack_factor * AME_NELEM * element_size_bits pass:[+] 7) / 8;
XReg alignment = <<ame:doc:func:min,min>>pass:[(]total_bytes, 4096);
if pass:[(]address % alignment != 0) {
  <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::LoadAddressMisaligned, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), address);
}
Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> tmp = <<ame:doc:func:read_memory_ame,read_memory_ame>>pass:[(]address, $encoding, total_bytes);
Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> impl = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_rm_to_impl,ame_xform_rm_to_impl>>pass:[(]tmp, dtype);
for pass:[(]U32 regno = 0; regno < group_size; regno++) {
  U32 bit_lo = regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE;
  U32 bit_hi = bit_lo pass:[+] AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE - 1;
  M[md pass:[+] regno] = impl[bit_hi:bit_lo];
}
}

[#ame:doc:inst:mls_st]
=== `mls.st`

Synopsis::
Load square from strided memory format

Mnemonic::
`mls.st md, (xs1), xs2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "xs2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x51,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Loads a square from memory in strided format into matrix register `md`.
+
The datatype is determined from `Md[md]`. For compute datatypes
(size >= `AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE`), one square is loaded. For packed datatypes
(size < `AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE`), `pack_factor = AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / sizeof(Md[md])`
squares are loaded and packed into a single M register.
+
The strided load accesses memory as a sequence of equally spaced segments, one segment per
row of the square. Each segment contains `sqrt(AME_NELEM)` contiguous elements, and
consecutive segments are separated by a byte stride supplied in integer register `xs2`.
Element `j` of row `i` is loaded from address
`base + i * stride + j * sizeof(Md[md])`.
+
The address must be aligned to the element size (`sizeof(Md[md])`) or 4KB,
whichever is smaller. Otherwise, `mls.st` raises a `Load Misaligned` exception.

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> xs2 = $encoding[24:20];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
XReg address = X[xs1];
XReg stride = X[xs2];
if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
  if pass:[(]md >= 16) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
}
Bits<32> dtype = Md[md];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_datatype_supported,ame_datatype_supported>>pass:[(]dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
U32 element_size_bits = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dtype);
U32 pack_factor = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_packed,ame_dtype_is_packed>>pass:[(]dtype) ? (AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / element_size_bits) : 1;
U32 group_size = (element_size_bits pass:[+] AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE - 1) / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
if pass:[(](md % group_size) != 0 || (md pass:[+] group_size) > AME_NUM_M_REGS) {
  <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
}
U32 sqrt_nelem = sqrt(AME_NELEM);
XReg element_bytes = (element_size_bits pass:[+] 7) / 8;
XReg seg_bytes = sqrt_nelem * element_bytes;
XReg alignment = <<ame:doc:func:min,min>>pass:[(]element_bytes, 4096);
if pass:[(]address % alignment != 0) {
  <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::LoadAddressMisaligned, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), address);
}
Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> tmp = 0;
for pass:[(]U32 seg = 0; seg < pack_factor * sqrt_nelem; seg++) {
  XReg seg_addr = address pass:[+] seg * stride;
  Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> seg_data = <<ame:doc:func:read_memory_ame,read_memory_ame>>pass:[(]seg_addr, $encoding, seg_bytes);
  U32 bit_lo = seg * sqrt_nelem * element_size_bits;
  U32 bit_hi = bit_lo pass:[+] sqrt_nelem * element_size_bits - 1;
  tmp[bit_hi:bit_lo] = seg_data[sqrt_nelem * element_size_bits - 1 : 0];
}
Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> impl = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_rm_to_impl,ame_xform_rm_to_impl>>pass:[(]tmp, dtype);
for pass:[(]U32 regno = 0; regno < group_size; regno++) {
  U32 bit_lo = regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE;
  U32 bit_hi = bit_lo pass:[+] AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE - 1;
  M[md pass:[+] regno] = impl[bit_hi:bit_lo];

Synopsis::

Mnemonic::
`mls.tst md, (xs1), xs2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "xs2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x52,"type":2}]}

Description::
Loads a square from memory in transposed strided format into matrix register `md`.
+
The datatype is determined from `Md[md]`. For compute datatypes

=== `mmax.ew`

Synopsis::
Elementwise maximum

Mnemonic::
`mmax.ew md, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....

....

Description::
Elementwise maximum of the tensors in `ms1` and `ms2`, stored into `md`.
Used for ReLU, pooling, and clipping.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md = np.maximum(ms1, ms2)
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];

Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----

Bits<32> src1_dtype = Md[ms1];
Bits<32> src2_dtype = Md[ms2];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::MAX1D, dest_dtype, src1_dtype, src2_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src1_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src1_dtype);
  U32 src1_nregs = src1_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src1_nregs == 0) {
    src1_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src1_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src1_nregs;
  U32 src2_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src2_dtype);
  U32 src2_nregs = src2_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src2_nregs == 0) {
    src2_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src2_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src2_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || ms2 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms1 % src1_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src2_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  for pass:[(]U32 e = 0; e < AME_NELEM; e++) {
    U32 src1_regno = e / src1_elements_per_reg;
    Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> src1_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms1 pass:[+] src1_regno], src1_dtype);
    U32 src1_hi_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src1_element_size) - (src1_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - 1;
    U32 src1_lo_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src1_element_size) - (src1_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - src1_element_size;
    U32 src2_regno = e / src2_elements_per_reg;

    U32 src2_hi_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src2_element_size) - (src2_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - 1;
    U32 src2_lo_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src2_element_size) - (src2_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - src2_element_size;
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src1_element_value = src1_value[src1_hi_idx:src1_lo_idx];
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src2_element_value = src2_value[src2_hi_idx:src2_lo_idx];
    <<ame:doc:func:ame_stage_m_element_write,ame_stage_m_element_write>>pass:[(]md, dest_dtype, e, <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::MAX1D, src1_dtype, src1_element_value, src2_dtype, src2_element_value, dest_dtype));

[#ame:doc:inst:mmax_ew_x]
=== `mmax.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise scalar maximum

Mnemonic::
`mmax.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x7,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Computes the elementwise maximum of a scalar constant from integer register `xs1` and
every element of square `ms2`, stored into `md`.
+
The type of the scalar is determined by `CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`. The scalar is converted
to the datatype of `ms2` before the operation and then applied to every element position `i`:
+
  `md[i] = max(xs1, ms2[i])`
+
If either `Md[md]` or `Md[ms2]` is uninitialized (zero), or the datatype combination
is not supported, `amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md = np.maximum(xs1, ms2)
----

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]

Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----

----

Bits<32> src_dtype = Md[ms2];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_scalar_op_supported,ame_scalar_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::MAX1D, dest_dtype, src_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src_dtype);
  U32 src_nregs = src_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src_nregs == 0) {
    src_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms2 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar = X[xs1];
  for pass:[(]U32 e = 0; e < AME_NELEM; e++) {

    Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> src_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms2 pass:[+] src_regno], src_dtype);
    U32 src_hi_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src_element_size) - (src_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - 1;
    U32 src_lo_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src_element_size) - (src_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - src_element_size;
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src_element_value = src_value[src_hi_idx:src_lo_idx];
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar_element_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_scalar_data_value,ame_scalar_data_value>>pass:[(]src_dtype, scalar);
    <<ame:doc:func:ame_stage_m_element_write,ame_stage_m_element_write>>pass:[(]md, dest_dtype, e, <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::MAX1D, src_dtype, scalar_element_value, src_dtype, src_element_value, dest_dtype));

Synopsis::

[#ame:doc:inst:mmov_a_m]
=== `mmov.a.m`

Synopsis::
Elementwise unconditional move from accumulator to M register

Mnemonic::
`mmov.a.m md, acc`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "acc","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0x1c,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Copies all `AME_NELEM` elements from accumulator `acc` to the M register
group `md`.
+
The element datatype of `acc` (from `Ad[acc]`) and `md` (from `Md[md]`)
must match; if they differ, `amestatus.UN` is set and `md` is left unchanged.
+
The destination `md` must satisfy the alignment constraint for its datatype:
`md` must be a multiple of the number of M registers consumed by one element.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md[:] = acc.flatten()
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> acc = $encoding[19:15];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<32> dest_dtype = Md[md];
<<ame:doc:func:ame_check_acc_index,ame_check_acc_index>>pass:[(]acc, $encoding);
Bits<32> src_dtype = Ad[acc];
if pass:[(]dest_dtype != src_dtype) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  U32 element_size_bits = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  if pass:[(]element_size_bits == 0) {
    CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
  } else {
    U32 dest_nregs = element_size_bits / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
    if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
      dest_nregs = 1;
    }
    U32 elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / dest_nregs;
    if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
    for pass:[(]U32 dest_regno = 0; dest_regno < dest_nregs; dest_regno++) {
      Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> result = 0;
      for pass:[(]U32 local_e = 0; local_e < elements_per_reg; local_e++) {
        U32 e = dest_regno * elements_per_reg pass:[+] local_e;
        U32 acc_bit_lo = e * element_size_bits;
        U32 acc_bit_hi = acc_bit_lo pass:[+] element_size_bits - 1;
        U32 reg_bit_lo = local_e * element_size_bits;
        U32 reg_bit_hi = reg_bit_lo pass:[+] element_size_bits - 1;
        result[reg_bit_hi:reg_bit_lo] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]Acc[acc], src_dtype)[acc_bit_hi:acc_bit_lo];
      }
      M[md pass:[+] dest_regno] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_rm_to_impl,ame_xform_rm_to_impl>>pass:[(]result, dest_dtype);
    }
  }
}
----

`mmov.m.a acc, ms`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "acc","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0x1d,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}

Description::
Copies all `AME_NELEM` elements from the M register group `ms` to accumulator `acc`.
+
The element datatype of `ms` (from `Md[ms]`) and `acc` (from `Ad[acc]`)
must match; if they differ, `amestatus.UN` is set and `acc` is left unchanged.

Bits<5> ms = $encoding[19:15];
----

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]

Bits<32> src_dtype = Md[ms];

    }

  if pass:[(]element_size_bits == 0) {

  } else {

    if pass:[(]src_nregs == 0) {

    }
    U32 elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src_nregs;
    if pass:[(](ms % src_nregs) != 0) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
    Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> result = 0;
    for pass:[(]U32 src_regno = 0; src_regno < src_nregs; src_regno++) {
      for pass:[(]U32 local_e = 0; local_e < elements_per_reg; local_e++) {
        U32 e = src_regno * elements_per_reg pass:[+] local_e;
        U32 acc_bit_lo = e * element_size_bits;
        U32 acc_bit_hi = acc_bit_lo pass:[+] element_size_bits - 1;
        U32 reg_bit_lo = local_e * element_size_bits;
        U32 reg_bit_hi = reg_bit_lo pass:[+] element_size_bits - 1;
        result[acc_bit_hi:acc_bit_lo] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms pass:[+] src_regno], src_dtype)[reg_bit_hi:reg_bit_lo];
      }
    }
    Acc[acc] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_rm_to_impl,ame_xform_rm_to_impl>>pass:[(]result, dest_dtype);
  }
}

=== `mmul.2d`

Synopsis::
Matrix multiply

Mnemonic::
`mmul.2d acc, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.

Computes the matrix product of `ms1` and `ms2` and stores the result into `acc`,
discarding any previous value in `acc`.
+
`acc[i,k] = sum over j of (ms1[i,j] * ms2[j,k])`
+
For the accumulating form, see `mmulacc.2d`.

----

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]

Bits<5> acc = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----

Bits<32> dest_dtype = Ad[acc];
Bits<32> src1_dtype = Md[ms1];
Bits<32> src2_dtype = Md[ms2];

  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src1_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src1_dtype);
  U32 src1_nregs = src1_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src1_nregs == 0) {
    src1_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src1_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src1_nregs;
  U32 src2_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src2_dtype);
  U32 src2_nregs = src2_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src2_nregs == 0) {
    src2_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src2_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src2_nregs;
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || ms2 >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms1 % src1_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src2_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);

        U32 a_lo = ((e_a pass:[+] 1) * src1_element_size) - (a_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) - src1_element_size;

[#ame:doc:inst:mmul_ew_x]
=== `mmul.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise scalar multiplication

Mnemonic::
`mmul.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0xd,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Multiplies every element of square `ms2` by a scalar constant from integer register
`xs1`, stored into `md`.
+
The type of the scalar is determined by `CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`. The scalar is converted
to the datatype of `ms2` before the operation and then applied to every element position `i`:
+
  `md[i] = xs1 × ms2[i]`
+
If either `Md[md]` or `Md[ms2]` is uninitialized (zero), or the datatype combination
is not supported, `amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written.

[source,python]
----
md = xs1 * ms2
----

Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];

Bits<32> src_dtype = Md[ms2];

  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {

  U32 src_nregs = src_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src_nregs == 0) {
    src_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms2 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);

    U32 src_lo_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src_element_size) - (src_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - src_element_size;
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src_element_value = src_value[src_hi_idx:src_lo_idx];
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar_element_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_scalar_data_value,ame_scalar_data_value>>pass:[(]src_dtype, scalar);

[#ame:doc:inst:mmulacc_2d]
=== `mmulacc.2d`

Synopsis::
Matrix multiply accumulate

Mnemonic::
`mmulacc.2d acc, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "acc","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x4a,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Accumulates the matrix product of `ms1` and `ms2` into `acc`.
+
`acc[i,k] += sum over j of (ms1[i,j] * ms2[j,k])`
+
`acc` is both a source and destination: its current value is read, the matrix
product is added, and the result is written back.
+
For the non-accumulating form, see `mmul.2d`.
For the subtracting form, see `mmulaccneg.2d`.

[source,python]
----
acc += ms1 @ ms2
----

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]

Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----

Bits<32> dest_dtype = Ad[acc];
Bits<32> src1_dtype = Md[ms1];
Bits<32> src2_dtype = Md[ms2];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::MMACC1D, dest_dtype, src1_dtype, src2_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src1_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src1_dtype);
