=== `mmulacc.ew`

Synopsis::
Elementwise multiply-accumulate

Mnemonic::
`mmulacc.ew md, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0xe,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Elementwise multiply-accumulate: multiplies `ms1` by `ms2` elementwise and accumulates
into `md`.
+
`md` is both a source and destination: the existing element is read, the product
`ms1[i] × ms2[i]` is added, and the sum is written back.
+
For each element position `i`:
+
  `md[i] = md[i] + ms1[i] × ms2[i]`

  U32 src2_nregs = src2_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src2_nregs == 0) {
    src2_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src2_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src2_nregs;

[#ame:doc:inst:mmulacc_ew_x]
=== `mmulacc.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise scalar multiply-accumulate

Mnemonic::
`mmulacc.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0xf,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Multiplies every element of square `ms2` by a scalar constant from integer register
`xs1` and accumulates the product into `md`.
+
`md` is both a source and destination: the existing element is read, the scaled value
`xs1 × ms2[i]` is added, and the sum is written back.
+
The type of the scalar is determined by `CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`. The scalar is converted
to the datatype of `ms2` before the operation and then applied to every element position `i`:
+
  `md[i] = md[i] + xs1 × ms2[i]`
+
If either `Md[md]` or `Md[ms2]` is uninitialized (zero), or the datatype combination
is not supported, `amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md = md + xs1 * ms2
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]

Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----

Bits<32> src_dtype = Md[ms2];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_scalar_op_supported,ame_scalar_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::MULACC1D, dest_dtype, src_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {

  U32 src_nregs = src_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src_nregs == 0) {
    src_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms2 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar = X[xs1];

    U32 src_hi_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src_element_size) - (src_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - 1;
    U32 src_lo_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src_element_size) - (src_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - src_element_size;
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src_element_value = src_value[src_hi_idx:src_lo_idx];
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar_element_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_scalar_data_value,ame_scalar_data_value>>pass:[(]src_dtype, scalar);

  }

=== `mmulaccneg.2d`

Synopsis::
Negated matrix multiply accumulate

Mnemonic::
`mmulaccneg.2d acc, ms1, ms2`

+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.

[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "acc","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x4b,"type":2}]}

Subtracts the matrix product of `ms1` and `ms2` from `acc`.

`acc[i,k] -= sum over j of (ms1[i,j] * ms2[j,k])`

product is subtracted, and the result is written back.

[#ame:doc:inst:mmulaccneg_ew_x]
=== `mmulaccneg.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise scalar negative multiply-accumulate

Mnemonic::
`mmulaccneg.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x11,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Multiplies every element of square `ms2` by a scalar constant from integer register
`xs1`, subtracts the product from `md`, and stores the result back into `md`.
+
`md` is both a source and destination: the existing element is read, the product
`xs1 × ms2[i]` is subtracted, and the result is written back.
+
The type of the scalar is determined by `CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`. The scalar is converted
to the datatype of `ms2` before the operation and then applied to every element position `i`:
+
  `md[i] = md[i] - xs1 × ms2[i]`
+
If either `Md[md]` or `Md[ms2]` is uninitialized (zero), or the datatype combination
is not supported, `amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md = md - xs1 * ms2
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<32> dest_dtype = Md[md];
Bits<32> src_dtype = Md[ms2];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_scalar_op_supported,ame_scalar_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::MULNACC1D, dest_dtype, src_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src_dtype);
  U32 src_nregs = src_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src_nregs == 0) {
    src_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms2 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar = X[xs1];
  for pass:[(]U32 e = 0; e < AME_NELEM; e++) {
    U32 src_regno = e / src_elements_per_reg;
    Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> src_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms2 pass:[+] src_regno], src_dtype);
    U32 src_hi_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src_element_size) - (src_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - 1;
    U32 src_lo_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src_element_size) - (src_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - src_element_size;
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src_element_value = src_value[src_hi_idx:src_lo_idx];
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar_element_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_scalar_data_value,ame_scalar_data_value>>pass:[(]src_dtype, scalar);
    <<ame:doc:func:ame_stage_m_element_op3,ame_stage_m_element_op3>>pass:[(]md, dest_dtype, e, AmeOperation::MULNACC1D, src_dtype, scalar_element_value, src_dtype, src_element_value);
  }
}

Synopsis::

[#ame:doc:inst:mmulat_2d]
=== `mmulat.2d`

Synopsis::
Matrix multiply with transposed A

Mnemonic::
`mmulat.2d acc, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "acc","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x4c,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Computes the matrix product of the transpose of `ms1` with `ms2` and stores the
result into `acc`, discarding any previous value in `acc`.
+
`acc[i,k] = sum over j of (ms1[j,i] * ms2[j,k])`
+
For the accumulating form, see `mmulatacc.2d`.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
acc = ms1.T @ ms2
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> acc = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
<<ame:doc:func:ame_check_acc_index,ame_check_acc_index>>pass:[(]acc, $encoding);
Bits<32> dest_dtype = Ad[acc];
Bits<32> src1_dtype = Md[ms1];
Bits<32> src2_dtype = Md[ms2];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::MMAT1D, dest_dtype, src1_dtype, src2_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src1_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src1_dtype);
  U32 src1_nregs = src1_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src1_nregs == 0) {
    src1_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src1_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src1_nregs;
  U32 src2_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src2_dtype);
  U32 src2_nregs = src2_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src2_nregs == 0) {
    src2_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src2_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src2_nregs;
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || ms2 >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms1 % src1_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src2_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  U32 N = <<ame:doc:func:sqrt,sqrt>>pass:[(]AME_NELEM);
  for pass:[(]U32 i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    for pass:[(]U32 k = 0; k < N; k++) {
      Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> sum = 0;
      for pass:[(]U32 j = 0; j < N; j++) {
        U32 e_a = j * N pass:[+] i;
        U32 a_regno = e_a / src1_elements_per_reg;
        U32 a_hi = ((e_a pass:[+] 1) * src1_element_size) - (a_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) - 1;
        U32 a_lo = ((e_a pass:[+] 1) * src1_element_size) - (a_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) - src1_element_size;
        U32 e_b = j * N pass:[+] k;
        U32 b_regno = e_b / src2_elements_per_reg;
        U32 b_hi = ((e_b pass:[+] 1) * src2_element_size) - (b_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) - 1;
        U32 b_lo = ((e_b pass:[+] 1) * src2_element_size) - (b_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) - src2_element_size;
        sum = <<ame:doc:func:ame_matmul_accumulate,ame_matmul_accumulate>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::MMAT1D, dest_dtype, sum, src1_dtype, <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms1 pass:[+] a_regno], src1_dtype)[a_hi:a_lo], src2_dtype, <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms2 pass:[+] b_regno], src2_dtype)[b_hi:b_lo]);
      }
      U32 e_d = i * N pass:[+] k;
      <<ame:doc:func:ame_stage_acc_element_write,ame_stage_acc_element_write>>pass:[(]acc, dest_dtype, e_d, sum);
    }
  }
}

[#ame:doc:inst:mmulatacc_2d]
=== `mmulatacc.2d`

Synopsis::
Matrix multiply with transposed A, accumulate

Mnemonic::
`mmulatacc.2d acc, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]

Description::
Accumulates the matrix product of the transpose of `ms1` with `ms2` into `acc`.
+
`acc[i,k] += sum over j of (ms1[j,i] * ms2[j,k])`
+
`acc` is both a source and destination: its current value is read, the transposed-A
matrix product is added, and the result is written back.
+
For the non-accumulating form, see `mmulat.2d`.

[#ame:doc:inst:mmulbtacc_2d]
=== `mmulbtacc.2d`

Synopsis::
Matrix multiply with transposed B, accumulate

Mnemonic::
`mmulbtacc.2d acc, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "acc","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x4f,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Accumulates the matrix product of `ms1` with the transpose of `ms2` into `acc`.
+
`acc[i,k] += sum over j of (ms1[i,j] * ms2[k,j])`
+
`acc` is both a source and destination: its current value is read, the transposed-B
matrix product is added, and the result is written back.
+
For the non-accumulating form, see `mmulbt.2d`.

----

Synopsis::

to the datatype of `ms2` before the operation and then applied to every element position `i`:

[#ame:doc:inst:mmulsub_ew]
=== `mmulsub.ew`

Synopsis::
Elementwise fused multiply-subtract

Mnemonic::
`mmulsub.ew md, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x16,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Elementwise fused multiply-subtract where `md` serves as both the multiplicand and the
destination.
+
`md` is both a source and destination: each existing element is multiplied by the
corresponding element of `ms2`, `ms1` is subtracted from that product, and the result
is written back. Note: this computes `ms1 - ms2 × md`, not `ms2 × md - ms1`.
+
For each element position `i`:
+
  `md[i] = ms1[i] - ms2[i] × md[i]`

md = ms1 - ms2 * md

[#ame:doc:inst:mornot_ew_x]
=== `mornot.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise scalar bitwise OR-NOT

Mnemonic::
`mornot.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x21,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Computes the elementwise bitwise OR of a scalar constant from integer register `xs1`
with the bitwise complement of every element of square `ms2`, stored into `md`.
+
The type of the scalar is determined by `CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`. The scalar is converted
to the datatype of `ms2` before the operation and then applied to every element position `i`:
+
  `md[i] = xs1 | ~ms2[i]`
+
If either `Md[md]` or `Md[ms2]` is uninitialized (zero), or the datatype combination
is not supported, `amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written.

[source,python]

md = xs1 | ~ms2
----

Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----

  U32 src_nregs = src_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;

  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;

    dest_nregs = 1;

  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {

[#ame:doc:inst:mpack_ew_x]
=== `mpack.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Pack one square into a slot of a packed M register

Mnemonic::
`mpack.ew.x md, ms1, xs1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x3d,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Inserts the single non-packed square group beginning at `ms1` into the sub-square slot selected by the
index in integer register `xs1` of the packed M register `md`, converting each element
from the non-packed datatype (`Md[ms1]`) to the packed datatype (`Md[md]`). The other
sub-square slots of `md` are preserved (read-modify-write).
+
`Md[md]` must be a packed datatype and `Md[ms1]` a non-packed datatype; otherwise
`amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written. The index must be less than
`pack_factor = AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / sizeof(Md[md])`; an out-of-range index raises an
`Illegal Instruction` exception.
The index is a fixed U32 unsigned integer control operand (the low 32 bits of
`X[xs1]`) and does not read
`CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`.
+
This is the single-square form of the pack (compress) direction of
<<ame:doc:inst:mconv_ew,mconv.ew>>; use it to pack one square at a time without holding
all `pack_factor` non-packed squares in registers simultaneously.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md[xs1] = pack(ms1)  # one sub-square, others preserved
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[24:20];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<32> src_dtype = Md[ms1];
Bits<32> dst_dtype = Md[md];
U32 index = X[xs1][31:0];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::CONV1D, dst_dtype, src_dtype, src_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else if pass:[(]<<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_packed,ame_dtype_is_packed>>pass:[(]src_dtype) || !<<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_packed,ame_dtype_is_packed>>pass:[(]dst_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  U32 dst_element_size_bits = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dst_dtype);
  U32 pack_factor = AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / dst_element_size_bits;
  if pass:[(]index >= pack_factor) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  U32 src_element_size_bits = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src_dtype);
  U32 src_group_size = src_element_size_bits / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src_group_size == 0) {
    src_group_size = 1;
  }
  <<ame:doc:func:ame_check_m_group,ame_check_m_group>>pass:[(]ms1, src_group_size, src_group_size, $encoding);
  <<ame:doc:func:ame_check_m_group,ame_check_m_group>>pass:[(]md, 1, 1, $encoding);
  Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> packed = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[md], dst_dtype);
  for pass:[(]U32 e = 0; e < AME_NELEM; e++) {
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> element = <<ame:doc:func:ame_read_m_element,ame_read_m_element>>pass:[(]ms1, src_dtype, e);
    U32 dst_lo = (index * AME_NELEM pass:[+] e) * dst_element_size_bits;
    U32 dst_hi = dst_lo pass:[+] dst_element_size_bits - 1;
    packed[dst_hi:dst_lo] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::CONV1D, src_dtype, element, src_dtype, element, dst_dtype);
  }
  M[md] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_rm_to_impl,ame_xform_rm_to_impl>>pass:[(]packed, dst_dtype);
}

[#ame:doc:inst:mprefixadd_col]
=== `mprefixadd.col`

Synopsis::
Elementwise column prefix-sum (inclusive scan)

Mnemonic::
`mprefixadd.col md, ms1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0x1f,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Computes an inclusive prefix sum (running sum) along each column of `ms1`, stored into `md`.
+
For each row `i` and column `j`:
+
  `md[i,j] = sum over k <= i of ms1[k,j]`
+
`md[0,j] = ms1[0,j]`, `md[1,j] = ms1[0,j] + ms1[1,j]`, and so on.
For the row prefix-sum form, see `mprefixadd.row`.
For the column max-scan form, see `mprefixmax.col`.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md[:,j] = np.cumsum(ms1[:,j])
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<32> dest_dtype = Md[md];
Bits<32> src1_dtype = Md[ms1];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::PREFIXADDCOL1D, dest_dtype, src1_dtype, src1_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src1_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src1_dtype);
  U32 src1_nregs = src1_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src1_nregs == 0) {
    src1_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src1_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src1_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms1 % src1_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  U32 N = <<ame:doc:func:sqrt,sqrt>>pass:[(]AME_NELEM);
  for pass:[(]U32 j = 0; j < N; j++) {
    U32 first_e = j;
    U32 first_src_regno = first_e / src1_elements_per_reg;
    U32 first_src_hi = ((first_e pass:[+] 1) * src1_element_size) - (first_src_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) - 1;
    U32 first_src_lo = ((first_e pass:[+] 1) * src1_element_size) - (first_src_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) - src1_element_size;
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> running = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms1 pass:[+] first_src_regno], src1_dtype)[first_src_hi:first_src_lo];
    <<ame:doc:func:ame_stage_m_element_write,ame_stage_m_element_write>>pass:[(]md, dest_dtype, first_e, running);
    for pass:[(]U32 i = 1; i < N; i++) {
      U32 e = i * N pass:[+] j;
      U32 src_regno = e / src1_elements_per_reg;
      U32 src_hi = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src1_element_size) - (src_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) - 1;
      U32 src_lo = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src1_element_size) - (src_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) - src1_element_size;
      running = <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::PREFIXADDCOL1D, src1_dtype, <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms1 pass:[+] src_regno], src1_dtype)[src_hi:src_lo], dest_dtype, running, dest_dtype);
      <<ame:doc:func:ame_stage_m_element_write,ame_stage_m_element_write>>pass:[(]md, dest_dtype, e, running);
    }
  }

=== `mrdexpacc.ew`

Synopsis::
Elementwise right-shift scale accumulate

Mnemonic::
`mrdexpacc.ew md, ms1, ms2`

Elementwise right-shift scale of `ms1` by a negative power of two given by `ms2`,
accumulated into `md`.

For each element, `md[i] = md[i] + ms1[i] * 2^(-ms2[i])`.

is added, and the sum is written back.
+
Complementary to `mldexpacc.ew`.

  if pass:[(]src2_nregs == 0) {
    src2_nregs = 1;

Synopsis::

Encoding::

Description::
Elementwise reciprocal of `ms1`, stored into `md`.
+
This instruction is defined only for floating-point datatypes. If the datatype of
`md` or `ms1` is not a supported floating-point type, `amestatus.UN` is set and no
register is written.
+
The numerical precision of the result is implementation-defined.

  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 dest_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / dest_nregs;
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }

  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {

[#ame:doc:inst:mreduceadd_col]
=== `mreduceadd.col`

Synopsis::
Elementwise column reduce (add)

Mnemonic::
`mreduceadd.col md, ms1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0x3,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank1-funct7},"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Reduces each column of `ms1` by addition and broadcasts the result to all rows of the
corresponding column in `md`.
+
For each row `i` and column `j`:
+
  `md[i,j] = sum over k of ms1[k,j]`
+
All rows in each output column receive the same value (the column sum).
For the row-reduction form, see `mreduceadd.row`.
For the column max-reduction form, see `mreducemax.col`.

md[:,j] = np.sum(ms1[:,j])
----

Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
----

} else {

  U32 src1_nregs = src1_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;

    src1_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src1_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src1_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms1 % src1_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  U32 N = <<ame:doc:func:sqrt,sqrt>>pass:[(]AME_NELEM);
  for pass:[(]U32 j = 0; j < N; j++) {

    U32 first_src_lo = ((first_e_src pass:[+] 1) * src1_element_size) - (first_src_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) - src1_element_size;
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> col_result = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms1 pass:[+] first_src_regno], src1_dtype)[first_src_hi:first_src_lo];

corresponding row in `md`.

For the column max-reduction form, see `mreducemax.col`.

[#ame:doc:inst:mrowgather_ew]
=== `mrowgather.ew`

Synopsis::
Elementwise row gather (take)

Mnemonic::
`mrowgather.ew md, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x36,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Elementwise gather: for each element in each column, uses the corresponding index from `ms2`
to select a row from the same column of `ms1`, and stores the result into `md`.
+
For each row `i` and column `j`:
+
  `md[i,j] = ms1[ms2[i,j], j]`
+
`ms2` provides integer row indices in the range `[0, sqrt(AME_NELEM))`.
An out-of-range index raises an `Illegal Instruction` exception.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md = np.take_along_axis(ms1, ms2, axis=0)
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]

Bits<32> src1_dtype = Md[ms1];
Bits<32> src2_dtype = Md[ms2];

    !<<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_integer,ame_dtype_is_integer>>pass:[(]src2_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src1_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src1_dtype);
  U32 src1_nregs = src1_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src1_nregs == 0) {
    src1_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src1_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src1_nregs;
  U32 src2_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src2_dtype);
  U32 src2_nregs = src2_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src2_nregs == 0) {
    src2_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src2_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src2_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 dest_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / dest_nregs;
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || ms2 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms1 % src1_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src2_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  U32 N = <<ame:doc:func:sqrt,sqrt>>pass:[(]AME_NELEM);

[#ame:doc:inst:mrowshift_ew_x]
=== `mrowshift.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise row shift by scalar offset

Mnemonic::
`mrowshift.ew.x md, ms1, xs1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x37,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Elementwise shift of each column of `ms1` by a signed 32-bit integer row offset `c` from
integer register `xs1`, stored into `md`.  This fixed-type control operand does not read
`CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`.
+
For each row `i` and column `j`:
+
  `md[i,j] = ms1[i+c, j]`  if `0 <= i+c < sqrt(AME_NELEM)`, otherwise
  `md[i,j] = zero(Md[md])`.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md = np.zeros_like(ms1)
if 0 <= c < ms1.shape[0]:
    md[:ms1.shape[0] - c, :] = ms1[c:, :]
elif -ms1.shape[0] < c < 0:
    md[-c:, :] = ms1[:ms1.shape[0] + c, :]
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[24:20];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<32> dest_dtype = Md[md];
Bits<32> src1_dtype = Md[ms1];

[#ame:doc:inst:mrowunzip_ew]
=== `mrowunzip.ew`

Synopsis::
Row unzip (de-interleave rows)

Mnemonic::
`mrowunzip.ew md, ms1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0xc,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}
....

Description::
De-interleaves the rows of `ms1` into the destination register pair (`md`, `md+1`).
+
Let `N = sqrt(AME_NELEM)`. For each row `k` in `[0, N)` and column `j`:
+
  `md[k,   j] = ms1[2k,   j]`   (even rows of the source)
  `md+1[k, j] = ms1[2k+1, j]`   (odd rows of the source)
+
The even-indexed rows of `ms1` are collected into `md`; the odd-indexed rows
into `md+1`.  `ms1` is treated as a conceptual `2N`-row matrix stored across
(`ms1`, `ms1+1`): rows `0..N-1` from `ms1`, rows `N..2N-1` from `ms1+1`.
+
`md` must be even-aligned.  Inverse of `mrowzip.ew`.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
src = np.concatenate((ms1[0], ms1[1]), axis=0)
md[0] = src[0::2, :]
md[1] = src[1::2, :]
----

Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
----

  U32 src1_nregs = src1_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src1_nregs == 0) {
    src1_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src1_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src1_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;

  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {

  if pass:[(](md % 2) != 0) {

[#ame:doc:inst:mrowzip_ew]
=== `mrowzip.ew`

Synopsis::
Row zip (interleave rows from two inputs)

Mnemonic::
`mrowzip.ew md, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x38,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Interleaves rows from two input registers `ms1` and `ms2` into the destination
register pair (`md`, `md+1`).
+
Let `N = sqrt(AME_NELEM)`. For each row `i` in `[0, N)` and column `j`:
+
  `md[i, j]     = ms1[i/2, j]`   when `i` is even  (i.e. `md[2k,   j] = ms1[k, j]`)
  `md[i, j]     = ms2[i/2, j]`   when `i` is odd   (i.e. `md[2k+1, j] = ms2[k, j]`)
+
`md` must be even-aligned.  Rows from `ms1` (A0) appear at even positions and rows
from `ms2` (A1) appear at odd positions within the register pair.
+
The destination register pair spans `2N` logical rows across (`md`, `md+1`):
rows `0..N-1` are in `md`, rows `N..2N-1` are in `md+1`.
+
Inverse of `mrowunzip.ew`.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
rows = np.empty((2 * ms1.shape[0], ms1.shape[1]), dtype=ms1.dtype)
rows[0::2, :] = ms1
rows[1::2, :] = ms2
md[0] = rows[:ms1.shape[0], :]
md[1] = rows[ms1.shape[0]:, :]
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----

Bits<32> src1_dtype = Md[ms1];
Bits<32> src2_dtype = Md[ms2];

  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src1_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src1_dtype);
  U32 src1_nregs = src1_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src1_nregs == 0) {
    src1_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src1_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src1_nregs;
  U32 src2_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src2_dtype);
  U32 src2_nregs = src2_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src2_nregs == 0) {
    src2_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src2_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src2_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {

  }

    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src2_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % 2) != 0) {

  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  U32 N = <<ame:doc:func:sqrt,sqrt>>pass:[(]AME_NELEM);
  for pass:[(]U32 k = 0; k < N; k++) {
    for pass:[(]U32 j = 0; j < N; j++) {
      U32 e_src = k * N pass:[+] j;
      U32 out_row_even = 2 * k;
      U32 out_reg_even = out_row_even / N;
      U32 out_local_even = out_row_even % N;
      U32 e_dst_even = out_local_even * N pass:[+] j;
      U32 src1_regno = e_src / src1_elements_per_reg;
      U32 src1_hi = ((e_src pass:[+] 1) * src1_element_size) - (src1_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) - 1;
      U32 src1_lo = ((e_src pass:[+] 1) * src1_element_size) - (src1_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) - src1_element_size;

      U32 out_row_odd = 2 * k pass:[+] 1;
      U32 out_reg_odd = out_row_odd / N;
      U32 out_local_odd = out_row_odd % N;
      U32 e_dst_odd = out_local_odd * N pass:[+] j;
      U32 src2_regno = e_src / src2_elements_per_reg;

      U32 src2_lo = ((e_src pass:[+] 1) * src2_element_size) - (src2_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) - src2_element_size;

Synopsis::

Encoding::

[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]

`md` or `ms1` is not a supported floating-point type, `amestatus.UN` is set and no
register is written.
+
The numerical precision of the result is implementation-defined.

[#ame:doc:inst:mselge_ew]
=== `mselge.ew`

Synopsis::
Elementwise predicated select (non-negative)

Mnemonic::
`mselge.ew md, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.

Description::
Elementwise predicated select using the non-negative sign of `ms1` to gate `ms2` into `md`.
+
For each element, `md[i] = ms2[i]` if `ms1[i] >= 0` (sign bit clear),
otherwise `md[i] = 0`.

`ms1` serves as the predicate register: any non-negative value is treated as true.
This allows comparison results or arithmetic sign values to be used directly
as predicates without normalization.

=== `msll.ew`

Synopsis::

Mnemonic::
`msll.ew md, ms1, ms2`

[#ame:doc:inst:msll_ew_x]
=== `msll.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise scalar logical left shift

Mnemonic::
`msll.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x23,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Logical left shift of every element of `ms2` by a scalar amount from integer register `xs1`, stored into `md`.
+
Only the low `lg2(EW)` bits of the scalar shift amount are used, where `EW` is the
element width in bits of `ms2`. The shift amount is interpreted as an unsigned bit pattern.
The X operand is a fixed unsigned integer control value and does not read
`CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
shamt = xs1 & (EW - 1)
md = ms2 << shamt
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<32> dest_dtype = Md[md];
Bits<32> src_dtype = Md[ms2];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::SLL1D, dest_dtype, src_dtype, src_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src_dtype);
  U32 src_nregs = src_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src_nregs == 0) {
    src_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 dest_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / dest_nregs;
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms2 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar = X[xs1];
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar_element_value = scalar[src_element_size - 1:0];
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> shift_amount = scalar_element_value & (src_element_size - 1);
  for pass:[(]U32 dest_regno = 0; dest_regno < dest_nregs; dest_regno++) {

    for pass:[(]U32 local_e = 0; local_e < dest_elements_per_reg; local_e++) {
      U32 e = dest_regno * dest_elements_per_reg pass:[+] local_e;
      U32 src_regno = e / src_elements_per_reg;
      Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> src_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms2 pass:[+] src_regno], src_dtype);
      U32 src_lo_idx = e * src_element_size - (src_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE);
      U32 src_hi_idx = src_lo_idx pass:[+] src_element_size - 1;
      U32 dest_lo_idx = local_e * dest_element_size;
      U32 dest_hi_idx = dest_lo_idx pass:[+] dest_element_size - 1;
      Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src_element_value = src_value[src_hi_idx:src_lo_idx];
      result[dest_hi_idx:dest_lo_idx] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::SLL1D, src_dtype, src_element_value, src_dtype, shift_amount, dest_dtype);
    }
    M[md pass:[+] dest_regno] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_rm_to_impl,ame_xform_rm_to_impl>>pass:[(]result, dest_dtype);
  }
}

Bits<32> src2_dtype = Md[ms2];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::SRL1D, dest_dtype, src1_dtype, src2_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src1_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src1_dtype);
  U32 src1_nregs = src1_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src1_nregs == 0) {
    src1_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src1_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src1_nregs;
  U32 src2_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src2_dtype);
  U32 src2_nregs = src2_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src2_nregs == 0) {
    src2_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src2_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src2_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 dest_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / dest_nregs;
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || ms2 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms1 % src1_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src2_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  for pass:[(]U32 dest_regno = 0; dest_regno < dest_nregs; dest_regno++) {
    Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> result = 0;
    for pass:[(]U32 local_e = 0; local_e < dest_elements_per_reg; local_e++) {
      U32 e = dest_regno * dest_elements_per_reg pass:[+] local_e;
      U32 src1_regno = e / src1_elements_per_reg;
      Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> src1_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms1 pass:[+] src1_regno], src1_dtype);
      U32 src1_lo_idx = e * src1_element_size - (src1_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE);
      U32 src1_hi_idx = src1_lo_idx pass:[+] src1_element_size - 1;
      U32 src2_regno = e / src2_elements_per_reg;
      Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> src2_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms2 pass:[+] src2_regno], src2_dtype);
      U32 src2_lo_idx = e * src2_element_size - (src2_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE);
      U32 src2_hi_idx = src2_lo_idx pass:[+] src2_element_size - 1;
      U32 dest_lo_idx = local_e * dest_element_size;
      U32 dest_hi_idx = dest_lo_idx pass:[+] dest_element_size - 1;
      Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src1_element_value = src1_value[src1_hi_idx:src1_lo_idx];
      Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src2_element_value = src2_value[src2_hi_idx:src2_lo_idx];
      Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> shift_amount = src2_element_value & (src1_element_size - 1);
      result[dest_hi_idx:dest_lo_idx] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::SRL1D, src1_dtype, src1_element_value, src2_dtype, shift_amount, dest_dtype);
    }
    M[md pass:[+] dest_regno] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_rm_to_impl,ame_xform_rm_to_impl>>pass:[(]result, dest_dtype);
  }
}

[#ame:doc:inst:msrl_ew_x]
=== `msrl.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise scalar logical right shift

Mnemonic::
`msrl.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x27,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Logical (unsigned) right shift of every element of `ms2` by a scalar amount from integer register `xs1`, stored into `md`.
+
Only the low `lg2(EW)` bits of the scalar shift amount are used, where `EW` is the
element width in bits of `ms2`. The shift amount is interpreted as an unsigned bit pattern.
The X operand is a fixed unsigned integer control value and does not read
`CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
shamt = xs1 & (EW - 1)
md = ms2 >> shamt  # logical
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<32> dest_dtype = Md[md];
Bits<32> src_dtype = Md[ms2];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::SRL1D, dest_dtype, src_dtype, src_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 src_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src_dtype);
  U32 src_nregs = src_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src_nregs == 0) {
    src_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 dest_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / dest_nregs;
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms2 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar = X[xs1];
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar_element_value = scalar[src_element_size - 1:0];
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> shift_amount = scalar_element_value & (src_element_size - 1);
  for pass:[(]U32 dest_regno = 0; dest_regno < dest_nregs; dest_regno++) {
    Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> result = 0;
    for pass:[(]U32 local_e = 0; local_e < dest_elements_per_reg; local_e++) {
      U32 e = dest_regno * dest_elements_per_reg pass:[+] local_e;
      U32 src_regno = e / src_elements_per_reg;
      Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> src_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms2 pass:[+] src_regno], src_dtype);
      U32 src_lo_idx = e * src_element_size - (src_regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE);
      U32 src_hi_idx = src_lo_idx pass:[+] src_element_size - 1;
      U32 dest_lo_idx = local_e * dest_element_size;
      U32 dest_hi_idx = dest_lo_idx pass:[+] dest_element_size - 1;
      Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src_element_value = src_value[src_hi_idx:src_lo_idx];
      result[dest_hi_idx:dest_lo_idx] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::SRL1D, src_dtype, src_element_value, src_dtype, shift_amount, dest_dtype);
    }
    M[md pass:[+] dest_regno] = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_rm_to_impl,ame_xform_rm_to_impl>>pass:[(]result, dest_dtype);
  }
}

[#ame:doc:inst:mss]
=== `mss`

Synopsis::
Store raw M register in implementation-defined format

Mnemonic::
`mss ms1, xs1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0x19,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Stores exactly one physical M register `ms1` into memory in the
implementation-defined register format.
+
The data written to memory is in the same implementation-defined format used by the
matching load instruction (`mls`). No datatype conversion is performed, and
the datatype in `Md[ms1]` is not interpreted.  Packed sub-squares resident in
`ms1` are transferred together, while a wide logical square requires one
`mss` for each physical M register in its group.
+
For portable interoperability with other implementations, use
<<ame:doc:inst:mss_rm,mss.rm>> or <<ame:doc:inst:mss_cm,mss.cm>>.
+
The address must be aligned to the physical M-register size or 4KB,
whichever is smaller. Otherwise, `mss` raises a `Store/AMO Misaligned` exception.



Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
XReg address = X[xs1];
if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
  if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
}
XReg impl_size_bytes = AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE >> 3;
XReg alignment = <<ame:doc:func:min,min>>pass:[(]impl_size_bytes, 4096);
if pass:[(]address % alignment != 0) {
  <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::StoreAmoAddressMisaligned, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), address);
}
<<ame:doc:func:write_memory_ame,write_memory_ame>>pass:[(]address, $encoding, M[ms1], impl_size_bytes);
----


<<<

[#ame:doc:inst:mss_cm]
=== `mss.cm`

Synopsis::
Store square to column-major format

Mnemonic::
`mss.cm ms1, xs1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": 0x1a,"type":2},{"bits":7,"name": {ame-enc-r2-bank0-funct7},"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Stores a square from matrix register `ms1` into memory in column-major format.
+
The datatype is determined from `Md[ms1]`. For compute datatypes
(size >= `AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE`), one square is stored. For packed datatypes
(size < `AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE`), `pack_factor = AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / sizeof(Md[ms1])`
squares are unpacked from the single M register and stored.
+
The data is written contiguously in column-major order: within each sub-square,
column `j` immediately follows column `j-1` with no padding. All elements are stored
unconditionally.
+
The address must be aligned to the total data size
(`pack_factor * AME_NELEM * sizeof(Md[ms1])`) or 4KB, whichever is smaller.
Otherwise, `mss.cm` raises a `Store/AMO Misaligned` exception.



Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
XReg address = X[xs1];
if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
  if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
}
Bits<32> dtype = Md[ms1];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_datatype_supported,ame_datatype_supported>>pass:[(]dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
U32 element_size_bits = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dtype);
U32 pack_factor = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_packed,ame_dtype_is_packed>>pass:[(]dtype) ? (AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / element_size_bits) : 1;
U32 group_size = (element_size_bits pass:[+] AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE - 1) / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
if pass:[(](ms1 % group_size) != 0 || (ms1 pass:[+] group_size) > AME_NUM_M_REGS) {
  <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
}
XReg total_bytes = (pack_factor * AME_NELEM * element_size_bits pass:[+] 7) / 8;
XReg alignment = <<ame:doc:func:min,min>>pass:[(]total_bytes, 4096);
if pass:[(]address % alignment != 0) {
  <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::StoreAmoAddressMisaligned, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), address);
}
Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> impl = 0;
for pass:[(]U32 regno = 0; regno < group_size; regno++) {
  U32 bit_lo = regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE;
  U32 bit_hi = bit_lo pass:[+] AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE - 1;
  impl[bit_hi:bit_lo] = M[ms1 pass:[+] regno];
}
Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> tmp = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_cm,ame_xform_impl_to_cm>>pass:[(]impl, dtype);
<<ame:doc:func:write_memory_ame,write_memory_ame>>pass:[(]address, $encoding, tmp, total_bytes);
}
----

[#ame:doc:inst:mss_st]
=== `mss.st`

Synopsis::
Store square to strided memory format

Mnemonic::
`mss.st ms1, (xs1), xs2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "xs2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x53,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Stores a square from matrix register `ms1` into memory in strided format.
+
The datatype is determined from `Md[ms1]`. For compute datatypes
(size >= `AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE`), one square is stored. For packed datatypes
(size < `AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE`), `pack_factor = AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / sizeof(Md[ms1])`
squares are unpacked from the single M register and stored.
+
The strided store writes memory as a sequence of equally spaced segments, one segment per
row of the square. Each segment contains `sqrt(AME_NELEM)` contiguous elements, and
consecutive segments are separated by a byte stride supplied in integer register `xs2`.
Element `j` of row `i` is written to address
`base + i * stride + j * sizeof(Md[ms1])`.
+
The address must be aligned to the element size (`sizeof(Md[ms1])`) or 4KB,
whichever is smaller. Otherwise, `mss.st` raises a `Store/AMO Misaligned` exception.



Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> xs2 = $encoding[24:20];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
XReg address = X[xs1];
XReg stride = X[xs2];
if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
  if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
}
Bits<32> dtype = Md[ms1];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_datatype_supported,ame_datatype_supported>>pass:[(]dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
U32 element_size_bits = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dtype);
U32 pack_factor = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_packed,ame_dtype_is_packed>>pass:[(]dtype) ? (AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / element_size_bits) : 1;
U32 group_size = (element_size_bits pass:[+] AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE - 1) / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
if pass:[(](ms1 % group_size) != 0 || (ms1 pass:[+] group_size) > AME_NUM_M_REGS) {
  <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
}
U32 sqrt_nelem = sqrt(AME_NELEM);
XReg element_bytes = (element_size_bits pass:[+] 7) / 8;
XReg seg_bytes = sqrt_nelem * element_bytes;
XReg alignment = <<ame:doc:func:min,min>>pass:[(]element_bytes, 4096);
if pass:[(]address % alignment != 0) {
  <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::StoreAmoAddressMisaligned, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), address);
}
Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> impl = 0;
for pass:[(]U32 regno = 0; regno < group_size; regno++) {
  U32 bit_lo = regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE;
  U32 bit_hi = bit_lo pass:[+] AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE - 1;
  impl[bit_hi:bit_lo] = M[ms1 pass:[+] regno];
}

[#ame:doc:inst:mss_tst]
=== `mss.tst`

Synopsis::
Store square to transposed strided memory format

Mnemonic::
`mss.tst ms1, (xs1), xs2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "xs2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x54,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Stores a square from matrix register `ms1` into memory in transposed strided format.
+
The datatype is determined from `Md[ms1]`. For compute datatypes
(size >= `AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE`), one square is stored. For packed datatypes
(size < `AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE`), `pack_factor = AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / sizeof(Md[ms1])`
squares are unpacked from the single M register and stored.
+
The transposed strided store writes memory as a sequence of equally spaced segments,
one segment per column of the square. Each segment contains `sqrt(AME_NELEM)` contiguous
elements, and consecutive segments are separated by a byte stride supplied in integer
register `xs2`. Element `(i,j)` of the register square is written to address
`base + j * stride + i * sizeof(Md[ms1])`, transposing the square during the transfer.
+
The address must be aligned to the element size (`sizeof(Md[ms1])`) or 4KB,
whichever is smaller. Otherwise, `mss.tst` raises a `Store/AMO Misaligned` exception.



Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> xs2 = $encoding[24:20];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
XReg address = X[xs1];
XReg stride = X[xs2];
if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
  if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
}
Bits<32> dtype = Md[ms1];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_datatype_supported,ame_datatype_supported>>pass:[(]dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
U32 element_size_bits = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dtype);
U32 pack_factor = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_packed,ame_dtype_is_packed>>pass:[(]dtype) ? (AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / element_size_bits) : 1;
U32 group_size = (element_size_bits pass:[+] AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE - 1) / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
if pass:[(](ms1 % group_size) != 0 || (ms1 pass:[+] group_size) > AME_NUM_M_REGS) {
  <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
}
U32 sqrt_nelem = sqrt(AME_NELEM);
XReg element_bytes = (element_size_bits pass:[+] 7) / 8;
XReg seg_bytes = sqrt_nelem * element_bytes;
XReg alignment = <<ame:doc:func:min,min>>pass:[(]element_bytes, 4096);
if pass:[(]address % alignment != 0) {
  <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::StoreAmoAddressMisaligned, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), address);
}
Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> impl = 0;
for pass:[(]U32 regno = 0; regno < group_size; regno++) {
  U32 bit_lo = regno * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE;
  U32 bit_hi = bit_lo pass:[+] AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE - 1;
  impl[bit_hi:bit_lo] = M[ms1 pass:[+] regno];
}
Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> tmp = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_cm,ame_xform_impl_to_cm>>pass:[(]impl, dtype);
for pass:[(]U32 seg = 0; seg < pack_factor * sqrt_nelem; seg++) {
  XReg seg_addr = address pass:[+] seg * stride;
  U32 bit_lo = seg * sqrt_nelem * element_size_bits;
  U32 bit_hi = bit_lo pass:[+] sqrt_nelem * element_size_bits - 1;
  <<ame:doc:func:write_memory_ame,write_memory_ame>>pass:[(]seg_addr, $encoding, tmp[bit_hi:bit_lo], seg_bytes);
}
}
----

[#ame:doc:inst:msub_ew_x]
=== `msub.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise scalar subtraction

Mnemonic::
`msub.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x19,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Subtracts every element of square `ms2` from a scalar constant from integer register `xs1`,
stored into `md`.
+
The type of the scalar is determined by `CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`. The scalar is converted
to the datatype of `ms2` before the operation and then applied to every element position `i`:
+
  `md[i] = xs1 - ms2[i]`
+
If either `Md[md]` or `Md[ms2]` is uninitialized (zero), or the datatype combination
is not supported, `amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written.

md = xs1 - ms2
----

Bits<5> ms2 = $encoding[24:20];
----

Bits<32> src_dtype = Md[ms2];

  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {

  U32 src_nregs = src_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;

  U32 src_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms2 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }

  for pass:[(]U32 e = 0; e < AME_NELEM; e++) {

    U32 src_hi_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src_element_size) - (src_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - 1;
    U32 src_lo_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src_element_size) - (src_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - src_element_size;
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> src_element_value = src_value[src_hi_idx:src_lo_idx];
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar_element_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_scalar_data_value,ame_scalar_data_value>>pass:[(]src_dtype, scalar);

=== `msublog2.ew`

Synopsis::
Elementwise bias minus log2

Mnemonic::
`msublog2.ew md, ms1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x47,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Elementwise complementary biased base-2 logarithm: subtracts the base-2 logarithm
of the absolute value of `ms1` from `ms2`, stored into `md`.
+
For each element, `md[i] = ms2[i] - log2(|ms1[i]|)`.
+
Useful for biased-exponent arithmetic, where `ms2` holds the bias value.
Complementary to `mlog2sub.ew`.

md = ms2 - np.log2(np.abs(ms1))

  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;

[#ame:doc:inst:munpack_ew_x]
=== `munpack.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Unpack one sub-square from a packed M register

Mnemonic::
`munpack.ew.x md, ms1, xs1`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "ms1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x3e,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Extracts a single sub-square, selected by the index in integer register `xs1`, from the
packed M register `ms1` and writes it to the non-packed M register group beginning at `md`, converting each
element from the packed datatype (`Md[ms1]`) to the non-packed datatype (`Md[md]`).
+
`Md[ms1]` must be a packed datatype and `Md[md]` a non-packed datatype; otherwise
`amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written. The index must be less than
`pack_factor = AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / sizeof(Md[ms1])`; an out-of-range index raises an
`Illegal Instruction` exception.
The index is a fixed U32 unsigned integer control operand (the low 32 bits of
`X[xs1]`) and does not read
`CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`.
+
This is the single-square form of the unpack (expand) direction of
<<ame:doc:inst:mconv_ew,mconv.ew>>; use it to unpack one square at a time and limit the
register pressure of fully expanding a packed register.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md = unpack(ms1)[xs1]  # one sub-square
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
Bits<5> ms1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> xs1 = $encoding[24:20];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<32> src_dtype = Md[ms1];
Bits<32> dst_dtype = Md[md];
U32 index = X[xs1][31:0];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::CONV1D, dst_dtype, src_dtype, src_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_packed,ame_dtype_is_packed>>pass:[(]src_dtype) || <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_is_packed,ame_dtype_is_packed>>pass:[(]dst_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms1 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  U32 src_element_size_bits = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]src_dtype);
  U32 pack_factor = AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE / src_element_size_bits;
  if pass:[(]index >= pack_factor) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  U32 dst_element_size_bits = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dst_dtype);
  U32 dst_group_size = dst_element_size_bits / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dst_group_size == 0) {
    dst_group_size = 1;
  }
  <<ame:doc:func:ame_check_m_group,ame_check_m_group>>pass:[(]ms1, 1, 1, $encoding);
  <<ame:doc:func:ame_check_m_group,ame_check_m_group>>pass:[(]md, dst_group_size, dst_group_size, $encoding);
  Bits<AME_MAX_SQUARE_SIZE> packed = <<ame:doc:func:ame_xform_impl_to_rm,ame_xform_impl_to_rm>>pass:[(]M[ms1], src_dtype);
  for pass:[(]U32 e = 0; e < AME_NELEM; e++) {
    U32 src_lo = (index * AME_NELEM pass:[+] e) * src_element_size_bits;
    U32 src_hi = src_lo pass:[+] src_element_size_bits - 1;
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> element = packed[src_hi:src_lo];
    Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> converted = <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::CONV1D, src_dtype, element, src_dtype, element, dst_dtype);
    <<ame:doc:func:ame_stage_m_element_write,ame_stage_m_element_write>>pass:[(]md, dst_dtype, e, converted);
  }
}

[#ame:doc:inst:mxor_ew_x]
=== `mxor.ew.x`

Synopsis::
Elementwise scalar bitwise XOR

Mnemonic::
`mxor.ew.x md, xs1, ms2`

Encoding::
+
[IMPORTANT]
This encoding is provisional and is not a final RISC-V allocation.
+
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x2b,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "md","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": {ame-enc-funct3},"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "xs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "ms2","type":4},{"bits":7,"name": 0x29,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Computes the elementwise bitwise XOR of a scalar constant from integer register `xs1`
with every element of square `ms2`, stored into `md`.
+
The type of the scalar is determined by `CSR[ame_scalar_dtype]`. The scalar is converted
to the datatype of `ms2` before the operation and then applied to every element position `i`:
+
  `md[i] = xs1 ^ ms2[i]`
+
If either `Md[md]` or `Md[ms2]` is uninitialized (zero), or the datatype combination
is not supported, `amestatus.UN` is set and no register is written.

md = xs1 ^ ms2
----

  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {

  U32 src_nregs = src_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]src_nregs == 0) {
    src_nregs = 1;
  }
  U32 src_elements_per_reg = AME_NELEM / src_nregs;
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]ms2 >= 16 || md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](ms2 % src_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> scalar = X[xs1];
  for pass:[(]U32 e = 0; e < AME_NELEM; e++) {

    U32 src_lo_idx = ((e pass:[+] 1) * src_element_size) - (src_regno * (AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE)) - src_element_size;

Writes zero to every element of the M register group `md`.
+
Typically used to initialize a tensor register before an elementwise sequence.


NumPy Equivalent::
[source,python]
----
md = np.zeros_like(md)
----

Decode Variables::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<5> md = $encoding[11:7];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<32> dest_dtype = Md[md];
if pass:[(]!<<ame:doc:func:ame_op_supported,ame_op_supported>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::ZERO1D, dest_dtype, dest_dtype, dest_dtype)) {
  CSR[<<ame:doc:csr:amestatus,amestatus>>].<<ame:doc:csr_field:amestatus:UN,UN>> = 1;
} else {
  U32 dest_element_size = <<ame:doc:func:ame_dtype_val_to_size,ame_dtype_val_to_size>>pass:[(]dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nregs = dest_element_size / AME_UNIT_DATATYPE_SIZE;
  if pass:[(]dest_nregs == 0) {
    dest_nregs = 1;
  }
  if pass:[(]AME_NUM_M_REGS == 16) {
    if pass:[(]md >= 16) {
      <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
    }
  }
  if pass:[(](md % dest_nregs) != 0) {
    <<ame:doc:func:raise,raise>>pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, <<ame:doc:func:mode,mode>>pass:[(]), $encoding);
  }
  Bits<AME_MAX_DTYPE_SIZE> zero_value = <<ame:doc:func:ame_op,ame_op>>pass:[(]AmeOperation::ZERO1D, dest_dtype, 0, dest_dtype, 0, dest_dtype);
  U32 dest_nelements = (dest_nregs * AME_MATRIX_REGISTER_SIZE) / dest_element_size;
  for pass:[(]U32 e = 0; e < dest_nelements; e++) {
    <<ame:doc:func:ame_stage_m_element_write,ame_stage_m_element_write>>pass:[(]md, dest_dtype, e, zero_value);
  }
}
----
